- **medkit-attending-debrief**: Reference for the DEBRIEF MODE that lives inside the `medkit-attending` Managed Agent. Use this skill when debugging why the agent emitted a particular `render_case_evaluation` payload, when modifying the rubric scoring logic, or when a citation appears unresolved in the UI. NOT used to author new rubrics — see medkit-rubric-author for that. ([bedriyan/medkit-app](https://github.com/bedriyan/medkit-app/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/medkit-attending-debrief) · ★337 · 5 copies)
- **medkit-guideline-curator**: Curate or refresh entries in `src/data/guidelines.ts` from authoritative society sources via WebFetch. Use whenever the registry needs a new condition (because a hero case rubric needs a citation that doesn't exist yet) or whenever existing entries should be checked for newer guideline versions. Designed to run on a `/loop 7d /medkit-guideline-curator` weekly schedule. Output is always `verificationStatus: "auto-fetched"` — a clinician must sign off "verified" by hand. ([bedriyan/medkit-app](https://github.com/bedriyan/medkit-app/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/medkit-guideline-curator) · ★337 · 5 copies)
- **medkit-rubric-author**: Author a `CaseRubric` for an existing `PatientCase` with PLAB2/RCGP/NURSE/SEGUE framework tags and citation discipline. Use whenever a case needs to graduate from the auto-derived fallback rubric to a hand-authored one for hero-level grading. Outputs an in-place edit of `src/data/polyclinicPatients.ts` (or `src/data/patients.ts`) inserting the `rubric: {...}` field. Every `clinical_management` criterion's `guideline_ref` MUST resolve in `src/data/guidelines.ts` — the rule is `cite, don't invent`. ([bedriyan/medkit-app](https://github.com/bedriyan/medkit-app/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/medkit-rubric-author) · ★337 · 5 copies)
- **medlabs-implement-contract**: Execute a settled MedLabs implementation or fix contract when the user or independent Reviewer has already supplied a verified finding, root cause, immutable behavior, exact replacement, or exact regression contract. Verify the source anchor, then implement exactly without reopening settled design. ([baonguyen-kobe/eiu-medlabs](https://github.com/baonguyen-kobe/eiu-medlabs/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/medlabs-implement-contract))
- **medlabs-release-preflight**: Release-only MedLabs preflight and production-verification procedure. Always delegates policy to docs/RELEASE.md and cannot deploy or mutate production without explicit current authorization. ([baonguyen-kobe/eiu-medlabs](https://github.com/baonguyen-kobe/eiu-medlabs/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/medlabs-release-preflight))
- **medlabs-ui**: Build or change any MedLabs page, component or style so it stays consistent with the rest of the app. Use for new pages, forms, tables, lists, drawers, badges, responsive fixes and UI reviews. ([baonguyen-kobe/eiu-medlabs-pilot](https://github.com/baonguyen-kobe/eiu-medlabs-pilot/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/medlabs-ui))
- **medlabs-vercel-preview**: Preview-only Vercel workflow for MedLabs rendered and user validation. It never authorizes production deployment, production aliases, production database mutation, merge, or implicit Git push. ([baonguyen-kobe/eiu-medlabs](https://github.com/baonguyen-kobe/eiu-medlabs/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/medlabs-vercel-preview) · 2 copies)
- **medlabs-verification-gate**: MedLabs change-aware completion and evidence gate. Select the smallest sufficient verification from actual diff and blast radius, permit prior PASS reuse only when impact is demonstrably unchanged, and never call an unexecuted check PASS. ([baonguyen-kobe/eiu-medlabs](https://github.com/baonguyen-kobe/eiu-medlabs/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/medlabs-verification-gate))
- **medlaybench-v-eval**: Evaluates the ability of medical vision-language models to align expert clinical terminology with patient-accessible layman language while preserving diagnostic accuracy. It measures lexical overlap, readability, clinical factuality, and zero-shot image-text retrieval performance. Use when the user wants to benchmark on MedLayBench-V, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Recall@K (R@1, R@5, R@10). ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/medlaybench-v-eval) · ★3)
- **medlearn-analysis**: 医学本科循证教学：比较临床研究、基础研究或指南的人群、研究设计、结局和效应量，解释P值、置信区间、证据质量和不同结论；资料不足时说明缺口，生成HTML与Word。适用于“为什么两项研究结论不同”“比较指南和证据”。 旧名称：medlearn-evidence-compare。 ([SiriHow/MedLearn-Skills](https://github.com/SiriHow/MedLearn-Skills/tree/HEAD/medlearn-analysis))
- **medlearn-bridge**: 为医学本科生从细胞、分子、生理与病理生理机制解释临床症状、体征、诊断依据和治疗原理；以用户材料为主，生成深度机制讲义。适用于“为什么出现这个症状”“从基础机制解释疾病”“从临床表现反推机制”。 旧名称：medlearn-basic-clinical-bridge。 ([SiriHow/MedLearn-Skills](https://github.com/SiriHow/MedLearn-Skills/tree/HEAD/medlearn-bridge))
- **medlearn-choice-questions**: 按用户提供的医学课程PPT、教材和教学范围生成章节专项题，重点支持A1/A2/A3/A4和多选题，提供逐选项解析、错题及疑题重练、交互HTML和Word；原功能亦可包含少量简答和病例分析题。适用于“按章节出选择题”“病例题训练”“错题重练”；整门课程综合模拟考试交给Final Exam。 旧名称：medlearn-assessment。 ([SiriHow/MedLearn-Skills](https://github.com/SiriHow/MedLearn-Skills/tree/HEAD/medlearn-choice-questions))
- **medlearn-explorer**: 面向医学本科生提供两阶段原始研究学习：先寻找并核实经典或前沿医学论文；获取用户上传的全文PDF后，按研究逻辑逐Figure/Panel讲解实验设计、图像、结果、统计和结论，输出独立HTML与Word。适用于“推荐经典原始论文”“逐图精读这篇论文”。 旧名称：medlearn-literature-explorer。 ([SiriHow/MedLearn-Skills](https://github.com/SiriHow/MedLearn-Skills/tree/HEAD/medlearn-explorer))
- **medlearn-final-exam**: 面向医学本科生根据课程大纲、教材、PPT及历年真题规划完整模拟试卷，包括限时交互考试、A/B平行卷、A1/A2/A3/A4/B1/X与主观题、标准答案、评分与考后诊断，输出HTML和Word。适用于“整门课程期末模拟考试”“根据多个章节组卷”。 旧名称：medlearn-mock-exam-v1.0。 ([SiriHow/MedLearn-Skills](https://github.com/SiriHow/MedLearn-Skills/tree/HEAD/medlearn-final-exam))
- **medlearn-integration**: 根据用户课程资料与指定医学专题，连接跨章节、跨学科的医学知识、因果机制及临床联系，制作可交互知识网络HTML和独立Word讲义。适用于“整合多个学科知识”“绘制机制知识图谱”。 旧名称：medlearn-integration。 ([SiriHow/MedLearn-Skills](https://github.com/SiriHow/MedLearn-Skills/tree/HEAD/medlearn-integration))
- **medlearn-notes**: 将学生提供的教学大纲、教材、PPT整理为准确、详尽、完整的医学本科章节学习笔记，按掌握/熟悉层级系统讲解知识点，生成Word等学习资料。适用于“整理章节笔记”“根据PPT做详细提纲”“复习讲义”；不用于主要生成题库或跨学科知识图谱。 旧名称：medlearn-chapter-map。 ([SiriHow/MedLearn-Skills](https://github.com/SiriHow/MedLearn-Skills/tree/HEAD/medlearn-notes))
- **medlearn-simulator**: 根据医学课程资料创建虚拟住院患者与动态病程，提供可离线运行的交互式HTML诊疗训练，包括病史、查体、检验、医嘱、查房、病情变化和复盘。适用于“模拟管床”“随机临床病例”“虚拟住院医师训练”。 旧名称：medlearn-clinical-practice。 ([SiriHow/MedLearn-Skills](https://github.com/SiriHow/MedLearn-Skills/tree/HEAD/medlearn-simulator))
- **medlearn-written-questions**: 根据医学课程教学大纲、教材及历史命题范式，设计简答题、论述题和病例分析题，生成详尽参考答案、原子采分点、评分细则与跨题去重检查。适用于“做主观题题库”“写简答论述标准答案”“病例分析采分点”。 旧名称：medlearn-written-exam。 ([SiriHow/MedLearn-Skills](https://github.com/SiriHow/MedLearn-Skills/tree/HEAD/medlearn-written-questions))
- **medlens**: Playbooks: `.claude/skills/&lt;name&gt;/SKILL.md` (mirrored in `.cursor/skills/`) Map: `docs/00-start/03-RULES_AND_SKILLS_MAP.md` · Provenance: `docs/00-start/04-SKILLS_PROVENANCE.md` ([itxSaaad/medlens-plus-app](https://github.com/itxSaaad/medlens-plus-app/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/medlens))
- **medley-review**: Review and propose patches for Bob Proulx's medley and the savannah-medley-scripts deploy config (the software that configures GNU Savannah hosts). Use whenever reviewing, hardening, or patching these repos, or matching their "house idiom" in new code. Covers the concrete medley/ Savannah shell + Perl conventions to replicate, the rule that classic-Perl and terse-shell style are intentional (match, never modernize), treating in-code TODOs as maintainer reminders rather than a worklist, and a threat-model-first gate that must clear before any change is proposed as a security/privacy "improveme… ([inputloop/vibrobeast](https://github.com/inputloop/vibrobeast/tree/HEAD/plugins/vibrobeast-skills/skills/medley-review) · 4 copies)
- **medlineplus-drug-info**: Query MedlinePlus for consumer-oriented drug and health-topic information. Accepts drug names, RxCUI codes, NDC codes, or ICD-10-CM diagnosis codes. Uses two free, keyless NLM APIs: the Web Service (keyword search) and MedlinePlus Connect (code-based lookup). ([BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills](https://github.com/BioTender-max/awesome-bio-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/drugclaw/medlineplus) · ★224 · 2 copies)
- **medlit-cli**: Use when the user explicitly invokes $medlit-cli to run the bundled stateful biomedical literature workflow: author and iteratively assess PubMed queries, select retrieved records, attempt open-access localization, extract evidence, and write an integrity-checked report. Does not validate medical correctness. ([starAndHonor/medsearch_skill](https://github.com/starAndHonor/medsearch_skill/tree/HEAD/plugins/medlit-cli/skills/medlit-cli))
- **medlit-cli**: Use when the user explicitly invokes $medlit-cli to run the bundled stateful biomedical literature workflow: accept a PICO JSON, build and run PubMed searches, fetch records, attempt open-access localization, parse supported sources, extract deterministic evidence, diagnose progress, and write and integrity-check a report. Does not extract PDF text or validate medical correctness. ([starAndHonor/medsearch_skill](https://github.com/starAndHonor/medsearch_skill/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/medlit-cli))
- **medlit-research**: 医学文献检索、批判性评价与综合分析。支持PubMed/Embase/Cochrane多数据库检索、PMC全文获取、AI辅助分析、研究质量评价、系统综述写作。使用场景：(1)检索多数据库医学文献，(2)自动获取PMC开放获取全文，(3)基于全文的深度批判性评价，(4)AI辅助PICO提取与质量评价，(5)辅助系统综述和Meta分析。 ([erongcao/medlit-research](https://github.com/erongcao/medlit-research/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **medlive-medical-qa**: 医脉通医学问答：向医脉通医学问答服务提问医学问题，获取带文献引用链接的完整答案，支持多轮追问、按文档/指南提问、历史问答回顾。当用户提出临床用药、适应症、剂量、禁忌、指南推荐、诊疗方案、病例分析等医学问题，或要求"照着这份文档/指南回答"、回顾之前的问答时使用；不要把它当通用搜索引擎或非医学问题助手。 ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/tree/HEAD/connectors/medlive-medical-qa/skills/medlive-medical-qa) · ★455)
- **medlive-medical-qa**: 医脉通医学问答。向医脉通医学问答服务提问医学问题并获取带文献引用链接的完整答案，支持多轮追问、文档问答（先用 medlive_upload_file 解析用户附件再提问）、历史答案回看与会话历史回顾（medlive_list_history）。当用户提出临床用药、适应症、剂量、禁忌、指南推荐、诊疗方案、病例分析等医学问题，或要求"照着这份文档/指南回答"、回顾之前的问答时使用。 ([ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills](https://github.com/ahang1598/doubao-workbuddy-qwenwork-skills/tree/HEAD/workbuddy/connectors/marketplace/connectors/medlive-medical-qa/skills) · ★103 · 3 copies)

- **medlock-lab**: Playbook for the MedLock Lab OpenClaw agent. ([Rojan-upreti/medlock_agent](https://github.com/Rojan-upreti/medlock_agent/tree/HEAD/CLI%20tools%20/nvdiahackathon/agents/lab/skills/lab) · ★1)
- **medlock-mongo-ops**: Host-side MongoDB is the system of record. Retrieval changes the lane. The sandbox is disposable. ([Rojan-upreti/medlock_agent](https://github.com/Rojan-upreti/medlock_agent/tree/HEAD/CLI%20tools%20/nvdiahackathon/agents/hospital-admin/skills/mongo-ops) · ★1)

- **medlog-ref**: Reference for the MedLog protocol — event-level logging of clinical AI: record fields, write-once event endpoints, enums, instrumentation and conformance. Use whenever MedLog is mentioned, or when asked to emit, parse, validate, or assess a MedLog record. For the whole logging system of an AI medical-device app (ops logs, audit trail, retention), use `aisamd-logging-guide`. - **medlvr-medical-vqa-eval**: Evaluates a model's ability to answer medical visual questions across diverse imaging modalities (CT, MRI, X-ray, etc.) and generalizes to out-of-domain benchmarks. It probes the model's capacity for latent visual reasoning and robust cross-modality transfer without relying on external tools or retrieval augmentation. Use when the user wants to benchmark on OmniMedVQA, SLAKE, VQA-RAD, PMC-VQA, MMMU (Health & Medicine), MedXpertQA, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports accuracy.
